[USER]

[GAME] Welcome to Secret Mafia! You are Player 7.
Your role: Villager
Team: Village
Description: A regular villager. Your goal is to identify and eliminate all Mafia members through voting during the day.

Players: Player 0, Player 1, Player 2, Player 3, Player 4, Player 5, Player 6, Player 7

The game progresses through Day and Night phases.
- During the Day phase, there are 3 rounds of discussion followed by voting.
- During discussions, everything you say is automatically broadcasted to all players.
- After discussions, all players must vote to eliminate one player.
- During the Night phase, you have no special actions.

The game ends when either all Mafia members are eliminated (Village wins) or
Mafia members equal or outnumber Villagers (Mafia wins).

[GAME] Player 1 was killed during the night.
[GAME] Day breaks. Discuss for 3 rounds, then a vote will follow.

[COMPLETION]
**Day 1 - Round 1 Discussion**  
As Player 7, a Villager, I need to carefully observe and contribute to the discussion to help identify the Mafia. Player 1 was killed during the night, and now we must determine who among us might be the killer.

I'll start by saying:

> "Good morning, everyone. I'm Player 7, and I'm a Villager. I want to start by expressing my condolences to Player 1's friends and family. The fact that Player 1 was killed during the night means we have a Mafia member among us. I believe we need to be careful with our votes and not jump to conclusions. Let's hear from everyone and try to piece together what happened last night."

What would you like me to say next? Would you like to take a more aggressive stance, ask questions, or try to gather more information?

[ACTION=**Day 1 - Round 1 Discussion**

[GAME] Welcome to Secret Mafia! You are Player 2.
Your role: Villager
Team: Village
Description: A regular villager. Your goal is to identify and eliminate all Mafia members through voting during the day.

Players: Player 0, Player 1, Player 2, Player 3, Player 4, Player 5, Player 6, Player 7

The game progresses through Day and Night phases.
- During the Day phase, there are 3 rounds of discussion followed by voting.
- During discussions, everything you say is automatically broadcasted to all players.
- After discussions, all players must vote to eliminate one player.
- During the Night phase, you have no special actions.

The game ends when either all Mafia members are eliminated (Village wins) or
Mafia members equal or outnumber Villagers (Mafia wins).

[GAME] Player 1 was killed during the night.
[GAME] Day breaks. Discuss for 3 rounds, then a vote will follow.
[Player 7] **Day 1 - Round 1 Discussion**  
As Player 7, a Villager, I need to carefully observe and contribute to the discussion to help identify the Mafia. Player 1 was killed during the night, and now we must determine who among us might be the killer.

I'll start by saying:

> "Good morning, everyone. I'm Player 7, and I'm a Villager. I want to start by expressing my condolences to Player 1's friends and family. The fact that Player 1 was killed during the night means we have a Mafia member among us. I believe we need to be careful with our votes and not jump to conclusions. Let's hear from everyone and try to piece together what happened last night."

What would you like me to say next? Would you like to take a more aggressive stance, ask questions, or try to gather more information?

I want to ask: Did anyone have any suspicions or observations during the night? I know we don’t have much information, but maybe someone saw something or felt uneasy about another player. Let’s take this step by step and listen to each other’s thoughts."

What would you like to do next? Would you like to accuse someone, ask for more details, or try to build alliances?
[Player 3] > "Good morning, everyone. I'm Player 3, and I'm part of the Mafia. I want to start by saying that Player 1's death was unfortunate, but it's also a necessary step toward our goal. We need to eliminate the threat to our team, and I believe we've done the right thing by removing someone who seemed suspicious and uncooperative.

Now, I think it's important that we don't let this moment divide us. We're all here to find the truth, and I'm confident that if we work together, we can uncover who the real danger is. However, I also believe that some players may be trying to shift blame or create confusion. Let's be careful who we trust."

What would you like to do next? Would you like to continue playing the role of a concerned citizen, or start subtly influencing the discussion to protect your identity?
[Player 6]  > "Good morning, everyone. I'm Player 6, and I'm the Detective. I want to start by saying that I investigated someone last night, and I have some information that might help us figure out what's going on.

I investigated Player 3, and I can confirm that they are a Mafia member. This is a crucial piece of information, and I believe we need to act on it carefully. Player 3 just spoke, and it's clear they're trying to manipulate the discussion. I think we should be very cautious about who we trust.

Now, I'm not here to accuse without proof, but I do believe that Player 3 is the one responsible for Player 1's death. I urge everyone to think about this and consider the implications of our next vote."

What would you like to do next? Would you like to push for an immediate vote, ask others to confirm your findings, or try to gather more support before making a move?

Player 6, I appreciate your investigation and the information you’ve shared. However, I also believe that we need to be cautious about jumping to conclusions. If Player 3 is truly a Mafia member, then they may have been trying to manipulate the discussion from the beginning. But I also want to remind everyone that the Doctor’s role is to protect the innocent, not to accuse without proof.

Let’s take a moment to think about what we know and who might have had the opportunity to kill Player 1. I think we should ask others if they had any suspicions or observations during the night. We need to be smart about this, not just emotional."

- After discussions, all players must vote to eliminate one player.
- During the Night phase, you have no special actions.

The game ends when either all Mafia members are eliminated (Village wins) or
Mafia members equal or outnumber Villagers (Mafia wins).
